We separate three signals. A task is diagnostic when its loss tracks global pretraining progress; it is teachable when its loss responds to its own token budget; and a data source transfers when including it improves","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-09","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_11548.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何区分合成数据在预训练中的不同作用（诊断、教学、迁移），并建立数据混合比例与下游任务收益的预测关系？","importance":"提出了一种系统性的方法来评估合成数据在预训练中的价值，将损失下降分解为诊断信号和教学信号，并验证了教学信号与下游迁移的强相关性，为数据配比（data mixture）提供了更科学的指导。","technical_approach":"将预训练语料分为 70% 的固定通用 Python 代码和 30% 的混合数据（包含 OpenCodeInstruct 任务和文献探针任务）。通过任务预算扫描（task-budget sweep），分离出三种信号：诊断（loss 随全局预训练进度下降）、教学（loss 随自身 token 预算增加而下降）和迁移（提升下游目标）。","experiments":"在受控预训练设置下，对 27 个任务进行了预算扫描，发现 14 个任务具有教学性（teachable）。精心策划的合成数据（10/12）比文献衍生的探针任务（4/15）表现出更强的教学性。教学性和下游迁移之间存在差异。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to distinguish the different roles of synthetic data in pretraining (diagnostic, teachable, transfer) and establish a predictive relationship between data mixture proportions and downstream task benefits?","importance":"Proposes a systematic approach to evaluate the value of synthetic data in pretraining, decomposing loss reduction into diagnostic and teachable signals, and verifying the strong correlation between teachable signals and downstream transfer, providing more scientific guidance for data mixture.","technical_approach":"Divides the pretraining corpus into 70% fixed general Python code and a 30% mixture of data (including OpenCodeInstruct tasks and literature probe tasks). Prefix-Based Extraction Beyond Isolated Prefixes","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) can expose memorized training sequences under prefix-based extraction: given a prefix from a training example, the model may assign high probability to the original continuation. In deployed systems, however, prefixes are rarely evaluated in isolation. They often appear together with instructions, retrieved documents, or other task-specific context, as in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). This motivates examining whether contextual conditioning mitigates memoriza","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-09","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_12085.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"研究 LLM 的 prefix-based 训练样本抽取在加入指令、检索文档或任务上下文后是否仍然成立。","importance":"贡献类型是预训练数据记忆与隐私分析。它把 memorization 从孤立 prefix 设置推进到更接近 RAG 和部署系统的 contextual conditioning 设置，对训练数据泄露、成员推断和数据去重策略都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文对每个 prefix-suffix 样本做 paired item-level 测量：同一目标 suffix 分别在空 prompt 和不同相关度的 retrieved context 下打分，比较目标续写的概率抽取强度。实验覆盖三个开源 instruction-tuned 模型，并分析哪些样本在上下文中保持可抽取，哪些样本因上下文而出现或消失。","experiments":"摘要未给出模型名称、参数量和具体抽取率。核心发现是上下文不会简单抹除记忆；可抽取记忆由 context-robust core 和 context-sensitive 部分组成，检索上下文会改变可抽取样本集合，而不是单调降低泄露风险。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether prefix-based extraction of memorized training sequences remains valid when prefixes are placed inside instructions, retrieved documents, or other task contexts.","importance":"This is a pretraining-data memorization and privacy analysis. At larger scale, teachers often get stronger while students remain smaller (compression/alignment distillation), which can worsen mismatch rather than wash it out. Using overlap as an observable, weakly scale-dependent diagnostic to switch sampling regimes is therefore unlikely to rank-reverse. If larger teachers increase coverage and overlap rises, the policy naturally switches back toward OPD, making it self-correcting across scale."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证“overlap阈值→选择Semi-OPD优于OPD”的可迁移判据，并量化其对蒸馏效率与后续SFT/RL可训练性的影响。","en":"Validate an overlap-threshold rule that selects Semi-OPD over OPD, and quantify effects on distillation efficiency and downstream post-trainability (SFT/RL)."},"data_sources":{"zh":"Proxy梯度：150M/350M/1.3B student；teacher固定为同架构更大或更强对齐版本（如 1.3B/3B/7B）。任务：混合通用指令+代码推理（保证teacher明显强于student）。为每个student先用固定prompt集生成一次离线rollouts（N=200k-1M responses）。","en":"Proxy ladder: 150M/350M/1.3B students; fixed teachers as larger/stronger-aligned variants (e.g., 1.3B/3B/7B).
